    the Company at the forthcoming 2018 annual general                                     人 民 幣208,027,000元），須 於 即 將 舉 行
    m e e t i n g . D i v i d e n d p a y a b l e t o S h a re h o l d e r s w i l l b e   之 二 零 一 八 年 股 東 週 年 大 會 獲 批 准 後，

     REMUNERATION SYSTEM                                                  薪酬訂立制度

     The Group ’ s remuneration policy has been determined                本集團的薪酬政策是根據其表現，本地的
     based on its performance, changes in the local consumption           消費水平變化和人力資源市場競爭狀況
     power and competition in human resources market. The                 釐定。該釐定的薪酬政策作為聘任不同崗
     remuneration policy so determined has become the basis               位僱員薪資水平的基準。每位僱員的薪資
     of determining the salary level of employees recruited               視乎僱員的表現、能力、任職條件及本公
     for different positions. The salary of each employee is              司的預定薪資標準而定。董事的酬金是由
     determined according to the employee ’ s performance,                薪 酬 委 員 會 經 參 考 本 公 司 的 經 營 業 績、

OVERVIEW                                                         概覽

The Board of Directors of the Company recognize the              本公司董事會深知在本集團的管理架構
importance of incorporating elements of good corporate           及內部監控程序引入優良的企業管治元
governance in the management structures and internal             素的重要性，藉以達致有效的問責性。本
control procedures of the Group so as to achieve effective       報告概述上市規則所載的企業管治常規
accountability. This report outlines the principles and the      守則（「守則」）的原則及守則條文，本集
code provisions of the Code on Corporate Governance              團已自其於二零零四年二月二十八日上
Practices (the “Code”) contained in the Listing Rules, which     市以來採納該守則及守則條文。
have been adopted by the Group since its listing on 28
February 2004.

in Appendix 10 of the Listing Rules as the standard for          準 守 則 作 為 董 事 進 行 證 券 交 易 的 標 準。
securities transactions by Directors. The Company has made       本公司已向全體董事作出特定查詢，而全
specific enquiries of all the Directors and all the Directors    體董事亦已確認彼等在二零一八年度一
confirmed that they have complied with the required              直遵守標準守則所載標準及其有關董事

regarding directors ’ securities transactions throughout the

RISK MANAGEMENT AND INTERNAL                                     風險管理及內部監控
CONTROL

The Board acknowledges that it is their responsibility for       董事會承認其須對風險管理及內部監控

Our objectives are to obtain reasonable assurance about          本行的目標乃對綜合財務報表整體是否

to any other person for the contents of this report.             任。合理保證為高水平的保證，但不能保
Reasonable assurance is a high level of assurance, but is not    證按照香港審核準則進行的審核總能發
a guarantee that an audit conducted in accordance with           現重大錯誤陳述。錯誤陳述可以由欺詐或
HKSAs will always detect a material misstatement when it         錯誤引起，如果合理預期其單獨或匯總起

considered material if, individually or in the aggregate, they   合財務報表所作出的經濟決定，則有關的
could reasonably be expected to influence the economic           錯誤陳述可被視作重大。
decisions of users taken on the basis of these consolidated
financial statements.

     that is sufficient and appropriate to provide a basis           審核憑證，作為本行意見的基礎。由
     for our opinion. The risk of not detecting a material           於欺詐可能涉及串謀、偽造、蓄意遺
     misstatement resulting from fraud is higher than for            漏、虛假陳述，或凌駕於內部控制之

      1. GENERAL                                                                            1. 概述

           Shandong Weigao Group Medical Polymer Company                                      山東威高集團醫用高分子製品股份
           Limited (the “Company”) was established and                                        有限公司（「本公司」）於二零零零年

           HKFRS 16 Leases                                                       香港財務報告準則第16號租賃


           HKFRS 16 introduces a comprehensive model for the                     香港財務報告準則第16號就識別租
           identification of lease arrangements and accounting                   賃安排以及出租人及承租人之會計
           treatments for both lessors and lessees. HKFRS 16                     處 理 引 進 一 個 綜 合 模 式。香 港 財 務
           will supersede HKAS 17 Leases and the related                         報告準則第16號生效後將取代香港
           interpretations when it becomes effective.                            會計準則第17號租賃及相關詮釋。

           HKFRS 16 distinguishes lease and service contracts on                 香港財務報告準則第16號根據所識
           the basis of whether an identified asset is controlled by             別資產是否由客戶控制來區分租賃
           a customer. In addition, HKFRS 16 requires sales and                  及 服 務 合 約。此 外，香 港 財 務 報 告
           leaseback transactions to be determined based on the                  準則第16號規定售後租回交易根據
           requirements of HKFRS 15 as to whether the transfer of                香港財務報告準則第15號有關轉讓
           the relevant asset should be accounted as a sale. HKFRS               相關資產是否應作為銷售入賬的規
           16 also includes requirements relating to subleases and               定 而 釐 定。香 港 財 務 報 告 準 則 第16
           lease modifications.                                                  號亦包括有關分租及租賃修改的規
                                                                                 定。

           Distinctions of operating leases and finance leases are               除 短 期 租 賃 及 低 值 資 產 租 賃 外，經
           removed for lessee accounting, and is replaced by a                   營租賃及融資租賃的差異自承租人
           model where a right-of-use asset and a corresponding                  會 計 處 理 中 移 除，並 由 承 租 人 須 就
           liability have to be recognised for all leases by lessees,            所有租賃確認使用權資產及相應負
           except for short-term leases and leases of low value                  債的模式替代。
           assets.

4. SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES                               4. 主要會計政策

   Statement of compliance                                         合規聲明


   The consolidated financial statements have been                 綜合財務報表乃按照香港會計師公
   prepared in accordance with HKFRSs issued by the                會頒佈之香港財務報告準則編製。
   HKICPA. In addition, the consolidated financial                 此 外，綜 合 財 務 報 表 載 有 聯 交 所 證
   statements include applicable disclosures required by           券上市規則及香港公司條例規定的
   the Rules Governing the Listing of Securities on the            適用披露。
   Stock Exchange and by the Hong Kong Companies
   Ordinance.

   Basis of preparation                                            編製基準


   The consolidated financial statements have been                 除於各報告期末若干金融工具按公
   prepared on the historical cost basis except for certain        平 值 計 量 外，綜 合 財 務 報 表 乃 以 歷
   financial instruments that are measured at fair values          史成本為基礎編製。
   at the end of each reporting period, as explained in the
   accounting policies set out below.

   Historical cost is generally based on the fair value of the     歷史成本一般以換取貨品及服務所
   consideration given in exchange for goods and services.         作出代價之公平值為基準。

   Fair value is the price that would be received to sell          公平值指市場參與者之間於計量日
   an asset or paid to transfer a liability in an orderly          期進行的有序交易中出售一項資產
   transaction between market participants at the                  所收取的價格或轉移一項負債所支
   measurement date, regardless of whether that price is           付 的 價 格，無 論 該 價 格 為 直 接 觀 察
   directly observable or estimated using another valuation        得出或採用其他估值技術作出的估
   technique. In estimating the fair value of an asset or a        計。在 對 資 產 或 負 債 的 公 平 值 作 出
   liability, the Group takes into account the characteristics     估 計 時，本 集 團 考 慮 了 市 場 參 與 者
   of the asset or liability if market participants would          於計量日期為該資產或負債進行定
   take those characteristics into account when pricing the        價 時 將 會 考 慮 的 該 等 特 徵。在 該 等
   asset or liability at the measurement date. Fair value          綜合財務報表中計量及╱或披露的
   for measurement and/or disclosure purposes in these             公 平 值 均 按 此 基 礎 予 以 確 定，惟 香
   consolidated financial statements is determined on such         港財務報告準則第2號以股份為基準
   a basis, except for share-based payment transactions            付款範圍內的以股份為基礎付款交
   that are within the scope of HKFRS 2 Share-based                易、香 港 會 計 準 則 第17號 租 賃 範 圍
   Payment, leasing transactions that are within the scope         內 的 租 賃 交 易、以 及 與 公 平 值 類 似
   of HKAS 17 Leases, and measurements that have some              但並非公平值的計量（ 例如，香港會
   similarities to fair value but are not fair value, such as      計準則第2號存貨中的可變現淨值或
   net realisable value in HKAS 2 Inventories or value in          香港會計準則第36號資產減值中的
   use in HKAS 36 Impairment of Assets.                            使用價值）除外。

           In addition, for financial reporting purposes, fair value             此外，就財務報告而言，公平值計量
           measurements are categorised into Level 1, 2 or 3                     根據公平值計量的輸入數據可觀察
           based on the degree to which the inputs to the fair                   程度及公平值計量的輸入數據對其
           value measurements are observable and the significance                整 體 的 重 要 性 分 類 為 第 一 級、第 二
           of the inputs to the fair value measurement in its                    級或第三級，概述如下：
           entirety, which are described as follows:

           •    Level 1 inputs are quoted prices (unadjusted) in                 •   第一級輸入數據指該實體於計
                active markets for identical assets or liabilities that              量日期可以取得的相同資產或
                the entity can access at the measurement date;                       負 債 於 活 躍 市 場 的 報 價（ 未 經
                                                                                     調整）；

           •    Level 2 inputs are inputs, other than quoted prices              •   第二級輸入數據指就資產或負
                included within Level 1, that are observable for the                 債直接或間接地可觀察的輸入
                asset or liability, either directly or indirectly; and               數 據（ 第 一 級 內 包 括 的 報 價 除
                                                                                     外）；及

           •    Level 3 inputs are unobservable inputs for the asset             •   第三級輸入數據指資產或負債
                or liability.                                                        的不可觀察輸入數據。

           The principal accounting policies are set out below.                  主要會計政策載列如下。

           Basis of consolidation                                                綜合基準


           The consolidated financial statements incorporate                     綜合財務報表包含本公司以及本公
           the financial statements of the Company and entities                  司 及 其 附 屬 公 司 所 控 制 的 實 體（ 包
           (including structured entities) controlled by the                     括結構性實體）的財務報表。當本公
           Company and its subsidiaries. Control is achieved when                司符合以下情況，即取得控制權：
           the Company:

           •    has power over the investee;                                     •   有權控制被投資方；

           •    is exposed, or has rights, to variable returns from              •   因其參與被投資方業務而獲得
                its involvement with the investee; and                               或有權獲得可變回報；及

           •    has the ability to use its power to affect its returns.          •   有能力以其權力影響其回報。

   The Group reassesses whether or not it controls an           倘有事實及情況顯示上述三項控制
   investee if facts and circumstances indicate that there      權 要 素 有 一 項 或 多 項 出 現 變 動，則
   are changes to one or more of the three elements of          本集團會重新評估其是否控制被投

   Leases (continued)                                              租賃（ 續）


   Leases are classified as finance leases whenever the            租約條款列明將所有權的絕大部分
   terms of the lease transfer substantially all the risks and     風險及回報轉移予承租人的租約分
   rewards of ownership to the lessee. All other leases are        類 為 融 資 租 約。所 有 其 他 租 約 則 被
   classified as operating leases.                                 分類為經營租約。

  The Group as lessor                                              本集團作為出租人


   Amounts due from lessees under financial leases are             融資租賃項下應收承租人的款項按
   recognised as receivables at the amount of the Group’s          本集團於租賃的淨投資金額確認為
   net investment in the leases. Financial lease income            應 收 款 項。融 資 租 賃 收 入 分 配 至 會
   is allocated to accounting periods so as to reflect a           計 期 間，以 反 映 本 集 團 有 關 租 賃 的
   constant periodic rate of return on the Group’s net             淨投資餘額的固定週期回報率。
   investment outstanding in respect of the leases.

           reflects the tax consequences that would follow from                  按照本集團於報告期末時預期收回

           amount of its assets and liabilities.

           is a legally enforceable right to set off current tax assets          資 產 與 即 期 稅 項 負 債 抵 銷，且 與 同
           against current tax liabilities and when they relate to               一稅務機關徵收之所得稅有關且本
           income taxes levied by the same taxation authority and                集團擬按淨額基準結算其即期稅項
           the Group intends to settle its current tax assets and                資 產 及 負 債 時，則 遞 延 稅 項 資 產 及
           liabilities on a net basis.                                           負債可互相對銷。

           Current and deferred tax is recognised in profit or loss,             即 期 及 遞 延 稅 項 於 損 益 內 確 認，惟
           except when it relates to items that are recognised                   倘即期及遞延稅項涉及於其他全面

           directly in equity respectively. Where current tax or                 務合併之初步會計方法而產生即期

                impact on the estimated future cash flows of                       金 流 量 構 成 不 利 影 響）發 生 時
                that financial asset have occurred. Evidence that                  維 持 信 用 減 值。金 融 資 產 維 持
                a financial asset is credit– impaired includes                     信用減值的證據包括有關下列
                observable data about the following events:                        事件的可觀察數據：

                (a)   significant financial difficulty of the issuer or            (a)   發行人或借款人的重大財
                      the borrower;                                                      困；

                (b)   a breach of contract, such as a default or past              (b)   違反合約（ 如違約或逾期
                      due event;                                                         事件）；

                (c)   the lender(s) of the borrower, for economic or               (c)   借款人的貸款人因有關借
                      contractual reasons relating to the borrower’s                     款人財困的經濟或合約理
                      financial difficulty, having granted to the                        由而向借款人批出貸款人
                      borrower a concession(s) that the lender(s)                        不會另行考慮的優惠；
                      would not otherwise consider;

                (d)   it is becoming probable that the borrower                    (d)   借款人將可能陷入破產或
                      will enter bankruptcy or other financial                           其他財務重組；或
                      reorganisation; or

   (iv) Write-off policy                                          (iv) 撇銷政策

         The Group writes off a financial asset when there              資料顯示對手方處於嚴重財困
         is information indicating that the counterparty                及 無 實 際 收 回 可 能 時（ 例 如 對
         is in severe financial difficulty and there is no              手方被清盤或已進入破產程序
         realistic prospect of recovery, for example, when              時），本集團則撇銷金融資產。
         the counterparty has been placed under liquidation             經考慮法律意見後（ 倘合適），
         or has entered into bankruptcy proceedings.                    遭撇銷的金融資產可能仍須按
         Financial assets written off may still be subject              本集團收回程序進行強制執
         to enforcement activities under the Group ’ s                  行 活 動。撇 銷 構 成 取 消 確 認 事

         advice where appropriate. A write-off constitutes              認。
         a derecognition event. Any subsequent recoveries

   (v)   Measurement and recognition of ECL                       (v)   預期信貸損失之計量及確認

         The measurement of ECL is a function of the                    預期信貸損失之計量為違約概
         probability of default, loss given default (i.e. the           率、違 約 虧 損（ 即 違 約 時 虧 損
         magnitude of the loss if there is a default) and               大 小）及 違 約 時 風 險 敞 口 之 函
         the exposure at default. The assessment of the                 數。違 約 概 率 及 違 約 虧 損 之 評
         probability of default and loss given default is               估乃基於歷史數據按前瞻性資
         based on historical data adjusted by forward-                  料 作 調 整。預 期 信 貸 損 失 的 預
         looking information. Estimation of ECL reflects an             估乃無偏概率加權平均金額，
         unbiased and probability-weighted amount that                  以各自發生違約的風險為權重
         is determined with the respective risks of default             確定。
         occurring as the weights.

         Generally, the ECL is the difference between all               一 般 而 言，預 期 信 貸 虧 損 為 根
         contractual cash flows that are due to the Group               據合約應付本集團的所有合約
         in accordance with the contract and the cash flows             現金流與本集團預期將收取的
         that the Group expects to receive, discounted at               現 金 流 之 間 的 差 額，並 按 初 始
         the effective interest rate determined at initial              確認時釐定的實際利率貼現。
         recognition. For a lease receivable, the cash flows            就 租 賃 應 收 款 項 而 言，釐 定 預
         used for determining the ECL is consistent with the            期信貸虧損所用的現金流與根
         cash flows used in measuring the lease receivable              據香港會計準則第17號租賃計
         in accordance with HKAS 17 Leases.                             量租賃應收款項所用的現金流
                                                                        一致。

   in funds under the control of trustees. All Hong Kong       團 之 資 產 分 開 持 有。所 有 加 盟 本 集
   employees joining the Group are required to join the        團之香港僱員均須參與強製性公積
   Mandatory Provident Fund Scheme.                            金計劃。

   The employees of the Group’s subsidiaries in the PRC        本集團之中國附屬公司之僱員均為
   are members of a state-managed retirement benefit           大陸政府經營之國家管理退休福利
   scheme operated by the government of mainland.              計 劃 之 成 員。該 等 附 屬 公 司 須 按 薪
   These subsidiaries are required to contribute 18% of        金成本之18%向退休福利計劃供款

   the benefits. The only obligation of the Group with         休福利計劃之唯一責任為作出指定
